#!/bin/sh
# xrdp X session start script (c) 2015, 2017, 2021 mirabilos

# Rely on /etc/pam.d/xrdp-sesman using pam_env to load both

# locale and the user environment properly.

if test -r /etc/profile; then

fi

if test -r ~/.profile; then
        . ~/.profile
fi

# start xfce4
startxfce4

# xrdp X session start script (c) 2015, 2017, 2021 mirabilos
# published under The MirOS Licence

# Rely on /etc/pam.d/xrdp-sesman using pam_env to load both

#!/bin/bash
# -------------------------------
# Clean XFCE + xrdp installation
# Restore Ubuntu Server to minimal state
# -------------------------------

# Stop xrdp service
sudo systemctl stop xrdp
sudo systemctl disable xrdp

# Remove xrdp completely
sudo apt purge -y xrdp
sudo apt autoremove -y
sudo apt autoclean

# Remove XFCE desktop and related packages
sudo apt purge -y xfce4 xfce4-goodies xorg x11-xserver-utils dbus-x11
sudo apt autoremove -y
sudo apt autoclean

# Remove user session configs
rm -f ~/.xsession
rm -f ~/.Xauthority

# Restore original startwm.sh (if exists)
if [ -f /etc/xrdp/startwm.sh ]; then
    sudo mv /etc/xrdp/startwm.sh /etc/xrdp/startwm.sh.bak
fi

# Close RDP port (3389) if ufw is enabled
if sudo ufw status | grep -q "Status: active"; then
    sudo ufw deny 3389/tcp
fi

# Reload firewall
sudo ufw reload

# Remove any leftover XFCE configs in /etc
sudo rm -rf /etc/xdg/xfce4
sudo rm -rf /etc/X11/Xsession.d/*xfce*

# Done
echo "âœ… XFCE, xrdp removed and port 3389 blocked. Your system is back to minimal Ubuntu Server state."

#!/bin/bash
# ==========================================
# Full XFCE + xrdp installation on Ubuntu 24 Server
# Custom RDP port, full XFCE desktop, UFW enabled
# ==========================================

# -----------------------------
# USER CONFIGURATION
# -----------------------------
CUSTOM_RDP_PORT=3390   # <-- change this if you want another port

# -----------------------------
# UPDATE SYSTEM
# -----------------------------
echo "ðŸ”„ Updating packages..."
sudo apt update && sudo apt upgrade -y

# -----------------------------
# INSTALL FULL XFCE DESKTOP
# -----------------------------
echo "ðŸ’» Installing full XFCE desktop..."
sudo apt install -y xfce4 xfce4-goodies xorg dbus-x11 x11-xserver-utils xterm

# -----------------------------
# INSTALL XRDP
# -----------------------------
echo "ðŸ” Installing xrdp..."
sudo apt install -y xrdp

# -----------------------------
# CONFIGURE XRDP CUSTOM PORT
# -----------------------------
echo "âš™ï¸ Configuring xrdp on custom port $CUSTOM_RDP_PORT..."
sudo sed -i "s/^port=.*/port=$CUSTOM_RDP_PORT/" /etc/xrdp/xrdp.ini

# -----------------------------
# SET XFCE AS DEFAULT SESSION FOR XRDP
# -----------------------------
echo "ðŸŽ¨ Setting XFCE as default session for xrdp..."
echo "startxfce4" > ~/.xsession
chmod +x ~/.xsession

# Clean startwm.sh to avoid session issues
sudo tee /etc/xrdp/startwm.sh > /dev/null <<EOF
#!/bin/sh
if [ -r /etc/profile ]; then
    . /etc/profile
fi
if [ -r ~/.profile ]; then
    . ~/.profile
fi

# Start XFCE
startxfce4
EOF

sudo chmod +x /etc/xrdp/startwm.sh

# -----------------------------
# ENABLE AND RESTART XRDP
# -----------------------------
echo "ðŸ” Restarting xrdp service..."
sudo systemctl daemon-reload
sudo systemctl enable xrdp
sudo systemctl restart xrdp

# -----------------------------
# CONFIGURE UFW FIREWALL
# -----------------------------
echo "ðŸ›¡ Configuring UFW..."
sudo ufw allow $CUSTOM_RDP_PORT/tcp

# Enable UFW if disabled
UFW_STATUS=$(sudo ufw status | grep -i "Status")
if [[ $UFW_STATUS == *"inactive"* ]]; then
    echo "ðŸš€ Enabling UFW..."
    sudo ufw enable
fi

echo "âœ… UFW updated. Current status:"
sudo ufw status

# -----------------------------
# FINAL STATUS
# -----------------------------
echo ""
echo "======================================"
echo "âœ… XFCE full desktop installed"
echo "âœ… xrdp installed and running on port $CUSTOM_RDP_PORT"
echo "âœ… XFCE session configured for xrdp"
echo "âœ… UFW firewall updated"
echo "You can now connect using your RDP client to port $CUSTOM_RDP_PORT"
echo "======================================"
ubuntu@pc-3:~$ chmod +x full_xfce_xrdp_custom_port.sh
ubuntu@pc-3:~$ ./full_xfce_xrdp_custom_port.sh
